# Houdini Node & Parameter Reference

Auto-consolidated from live Houdini **21.0.671** node/parameter probes -- exact ground truth for this version (nothing here is guessed; every param name was read from a live node). This file is the human-readable archive backing the `node_reference` MCP tool; the structured source of record is `houdini_nodes.json` in this folder.

## Grep grammar

Every parameter is one line, so three different greps all work:

- `grep '\[groupcreate\]'` -- every param of a node
- `grep ' grouptype '` -- every node that has a param named `grouptype`
- `grep ':menu'` -- every menu param in the whole reference

Line format:

```
[<nodetype>] <paramname> :<type>[ :menu(tok1|tok2|...)][ :vec<N>][ :range(min..max)][ :def=<default>]
```

`:type` is lowercase (string/float/int/toggle/menu/ramp/button/folder/... or `unknown` when the probe captured only the name). `:menu(...)` lists selectable tokens (capped at 24, then `...`). `:vec<N>` appears only for multi-component params. `:range(min..max)` and `:def=<default>` appear only when the probe captured them.

[SOP](#sop) | [OBJ (Object)](#obj-object) | [LOP](#lop) | [Driver](#driver) | [COP](#cop)
